navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n      navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy).then(() => {\n        setCopiedId(identifier);\n        showToast(`「${textToCopy}」をコピーしました`);\n        window.setTimeout(() => {\n          setCopiedId(null);\n        }, 1800);\n      }).catch(() => {\n        fallbackCopy(textToCopy, identifier);\n      });\n    } else {\n      fallbackCopy(textToCopy, identifier);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const fallbackCopy = (text: string, identifier: string) => {\n    const textArea = document.createElement('textarea');\n    textArea.value = History,\n  BookOpen,\n  Info,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { LengthUnitId, HistoryRecord } from './types';\nimport {\n  LENGTH_UNITS,\n  LENGTH_UNIT_LIST,\n  PRESETS,\n  convertLength,\n [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // 履歴\n  const [historyList, setHistoryList] = useState<HistoryRecord[]>(() => [\n    {\n      id: 'init-1',\n      inputValue: 1,\n      fromUnit: 'm',\n      toUnit: 'cm',\n      resultValue: 100,\n      timestamp: '12:00',\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'init-2',\n      inputValue: 1,\n      fromUnit: 'in',\n      toUnit: 'mm',\n      resultValue: 25.4,\n      timestamp: '12:01',\n    },\n  ]);\n\n  const uniqueId = useId();\n\n  const toUnit: LengthUnitId\n): number => {\n  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;\n  if (fromUnit === toUnit) return value;\n\n  const fromDef = LENGTH_UNITS[fromUnit];\n return numberFormatter.format(rounded);\n};","language":"typescript"}

|| !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) return 0;\n  if (fromUnit === toUnit) return value;\n\n  const fromDef = LENGTH_UNITS[fromUnit];\n  const toDef = LENGTH_UNITS[toUnit];\n\n  if (!fromDef || 1e7 || absVal < 1e-6) {\n    return val.toExponential(4);\n  }\n\n  const rounded = Number(Math.round(Number(`${val}e8`)) + 'e-8');\n  return History,\n  BookOpen,\n  Info,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { LengthUnitId, HistoryRecord } from './types';\nimport {\n  LENGTH_UNITS,\n  LENGTH_UNIT_LIST,\n  PRESETS,\n  convertLength,\n !Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;\n  if (fromUnit === toUnit) return value;\n\n  const fromDef = LENGTH_UNITS[fromUnit];\n  const toDef = LENGTH_UNITS[toUnit];\n\n  if (!fromDef || !toDef) return 0;\n};\n\nconst numberFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP', {\n  maximumFractionDigits: 8,\n  useGrouping: true,\n});\n\n/**\n * 有効桁数を適切に調整してフォーマットする関数\n */\nexport const formatLengthValue = Check,\n  RotateCcw,\n  Sparkles,\n  History,\n  BookOpen,\n  Info,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { LengthUnitId, HistoryRecord } from './types';\nimport {\n  LENGTH_UNITS,\n  LENGTH_UNIT_LIST,\n  PRESETS,\n  convertLength,\n  formatLengthValue,\n} from './constants';\n\ntype Mode = 'all' | 'pair';\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('all');\n  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState<string>('1');\n  const [baseUnit, setBaseUnit] = useState<LengthUnitId>('m');\n\n  // 個別変換モード用\n  const [targetUnit, setTargetUnit] =  id: 'init-1',\n      inputValue: 1,\n      fromUnit: 'm',\n      toUnit: 'cm',\n      resultValue: 100,\n      timestamp: '12:00',\n      createdAt: Date.now() - 60000,\n    },\n    {\n      id: parseFloat(inputValue);\n  const numericValue = isNaN(parsedValue) ? 0 : parsedValue;\n\n  const showToast = (message: string) => {\n    if (toastTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n      clearTimeout(toastTimeoutRef.current);\n    }\n    setToastMessage(message);\n    toastTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setToastMessage(null);\n      toastTimeoutRef.current = null;\n    }, 2500);\n  };\n\n  const handleCopy = (textToCopy: string, identifier: string) => {\n    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n      navigator.clipboard\n        .writeText(textToCopy)\n        .then(() => {\n          if (copyTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n            clearTimeout(copyTimeoutRef.current);\n          }\n          setCopiedId(identifier);\n          showToast(`「${textToCopy}」をコピーしました`);\n          copyTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n            setCopiedId(null);\n            copyTimeoutRef.current = null;\n          }, (from: LengthUnitId, to: LengthUnitId, inputVal: number, resultVal: number) => {\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeStr = `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2,   latest.fromUnit === from &&\n        latest.toUnit === to &&\n        latest.inputValue === inputVal &&\n        latest.resultValue === resultVal\n      ) {\n        return prev;\n      }\n\n      const recordId =\n        typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID\n          ? crypto.randomUUID()\n          : `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n\n      const newRecord: HistoryRecord = {\n        id: recordId,\n        inputValue: inputVal,\n        fromUnit: from,\n        toUnit: to,\n        resultValue: resultVal,\n        timestamp: timeStr,\n label: '標準的な定規 (30cm)', value: 30, unit: 'cm' },\n  { id: 'preset-meter', label: '1メートル (1m)', value: 1, unit: 'm' },\n  { id: 'preset-height', label: '成人平均身長 (170cm)', value: 170, unit: 'cm' },\n  { id: 'preset-inch', label: '1インチ (25.4mm)', value: 25.4, unit: 'mm' },\n  { id: 'preset-shoe', label: '成人男性の足サイズ (10in)', value: 10, unit: 'in' },\n  { id: 'preset-foot', label: '1フィート (30.48cm)', value: 30.48, unit: 'cm' },\n  { id: 'preset-height-ft', label: '一般的な身長目安 (5.7ft)', value: 5.7, unit: 'ft' },\n  { id: 'preset-marathon', label: 'フルマラソン (42.195km)', value: 42.195, unit: 'km' },\n];\n\n/**\n * 単位間の換算値を計算する関数\n */\nexport const convertLength = (\n  value: number,\n  fromUnit: LengthUnitId,\n  toUnit: LengthUnitId\n): number => {\n  if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value)) return 0;\n  if (fromUnit === toUnit) return value;\n\n  const fromDef = LENGTH_UNITS[fromUnit];\n  const toDef = LENGTH_UNITS[toUnit];\n\n  if (!fromDef || !toDef || !toDef.toMeters || toDef.toMeters <= 0) return 0;\n\n  const meters = value * fromDef.toMeters;\n  const rawResult = meters / toDef.toMeters;\n\n  if (!Number.isFinite(rawResult)) return 0;\n\n  // 浮動小数点演算の丸め誤差を抑える処理\n  return Math.round(rawResult * 1e12) / 1e12;\n};\n\nconst numberFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat('ja-JP', {\n  maximumFractionDigits: 10,\n  useGrouping: true,\n});\n\n/**\n * 有効桁数を適切に調整してフォーマットする関数\n */\nexport const formatLengthValue = (val: number): string => {\n  if (typeof val !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(val)) return '0';\n  if (val === 0) return '0';\n\n  const absVal = Math.abs(val);\n\n  // 一般的な微細寸法（μmオーダーや0.001mmなど）も通常表記できるよう、極端に大きい・小さい場合のみ指数表記にする\n  if (absVal >= 1e12 || absVal < 1e-9) {\n    return val.toExponential(4);\n  }\n\n  return numberFormatter.format(val);\n};","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/index.css","content":":root {\n  --primary-color: #2563eb;\n  --primary-hover: #1d4ed8;\n  --primary-light: #eff6ff;\n History,\n  BookOpen,\n  Info,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { LengthUnitId, HistoryRecord } from './types';\nimport {\n  LENGTH_UNITS,\n  LENGTH_UNIT_LIST,\n  PRESETS,\n  convertLength,\n  formatLengthValue,\n} from './constants';\n\ntype Mode = 'all' | 'pair';\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('all');\n  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState<string>('1');\n  const [baseUnit, setBaseUnit] = useState<LengthUnitId>('m');\n\n  // 個別変換モード用\n  const [targetUnit, setTargetUnit] = useState<LengthUnitId>('cm');\n\n  // コピー状態とトースト\n  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const toastTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const copyTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  // アンマウント時のタイマー解除\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      if (toastTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n        clearTimeout(toastTimeoutRef.current);\n      }\n      if (copyTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n        clearTimeout(copyTimeoutRef.current);\n      }\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // 履歴初期データ\n  const [historyList, setHistoryList] = useState<HistoryRecord[]>(() => [\n    {\n      id: 'init-1',\n      inputValue: 1,\n      fromUnit: 'm',\n      toUnit: 'cm',\n      resultValue: 100,\n      timestamp: '12:00',\n      createdAt: Date.now() - 60000,\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'init-2',\n      inputValue: 1,\n      fromUnit: 'in',\n      toUnit: 'mm',\n      resultValue: 25.4,\n      timestamp: '12:01',\n      createdAt: Date.now(),\n    },\n  ]);\n\n  const parsedValue = parseFloat(inputValue);\n  const numericValue = isNaN(parsedValue) ? 0 : parsedValue;\n\n  const showToast = (message: string) => {\n    if (toastTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n      clearTimeout(toastTimeoutRef.current);\n    }\n    setToastMessage(message);\n    toastTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setToastMessage(null);\n      toastTimeoutRef.current = null;\n    }, 2500);\n  };\n\n  const handleCopy = (textToCopy: string, identifier: string) => {\n    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n      navigator.clipboard\n        .writeText(textToCopy)\n        .then(() => {\n          if (copyTimeoutRef.current !== null) {\n            clearTimeout(copyTimeoutRef.current);\n          }\n          setCopiedId(identifier);\n          showToast(`「${textToCopy}」をコピーしました`);\n          copyTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n            setCopiedId(null);\n            copyTimeoutRef.current = null;\n          }, 1800);\n        })\n        .catch(() => {\n          showToast('コピーに失敗しました');\n        });\n    } else {\n      showToast('お使いの環境ではクリップボードへのアクセスが許可されていません');\n    }\n  };\n\n  const addHistory = (from: LengthUnitId, to: LengthUnitId, inputVal: number, resultVal: number) => {\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeStr = `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(now.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0')}`;\n\n    setHistoryList((prev) => {\n      const latest = prev[0];\n      if (\n        latest &&\n        latest.fromUnit === from &&\n        latest.toUnit === to &&\n        latest.inputValue === inputVal &&\n        latest.resultValue === resultVal\n      ) {\n        return prev;\n      }\n\n      const recordId =\n        typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID\n          ? crypto.randomUUID()\n          : `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n\n      const newRecord: HistoryRecord = {\n        id: recordId,\n        inputValue: inputVal,\n        fromUnit: from,\n        toUnit: to,\n        resultValue: resultVal,\n        timestamp: timeStr,\n        createdAt: Date.now(),\n      };\n      return [newRecord, ...prev.slice(0, 19)];\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handlePresetSelect = (value: number, unit: LengthUnitId) => {\n    setInputValue(value.toString());\n    setBaseUnit(unit);\n    // モードに関わらず整合性を持たせて履歴に追加\n    const destUnit = unit === targetUnit ? (unit === 'cm' ?
